function OnRep_SittingAtTerminal  [FUNC_BlueprintCallable | FUNC_BlueprintEvent]
            if not (self.SittingAtTerminal) goto @133
            CallFunc_GetAnimInstance_ReturnValue = self.Hazmat_Idle.SkeletalMeshComponent.GetAnimInstance()
            CallFunc_Montage_Play_ReturnValue = CallFunc_GetAnimInstance_ReturnValue.AnimInstance.Montage_Play(Obj<P_Hazmat2_Terminal>, 1.0, 0, 0.0, true)
            goto @221
    @133:   CallFunc_GetAnimInstance_ReturnValue = self.Hazmat_Idle.SkeletalMeshComponent.GetAnimInstance()
            CallFunc_GetAnimInstance_ReturnValue.AnimInstance.Montage_Stop(0.0, Obj<P_Hazmat2_Terminal>)
    @221:   return

function SwayWeaponArms  [FUNC_Public | FUNC_BlueprintCallable | FUNC_BlueprintEvent]
            CallFunc_K2_GetComponentRotation_ReturnValue = self.FirstPersonCamera.SceneComponent.K2_GetComponentRotation()
            self.CamRotationCurrent = CallFunc_K2_GetComponentRotation_ReturnValue
            CallFunc_GetWorldDeltaSeconds_ReturnValue = GameplayStatics.GetWorldDeltaSeconds(self)
            CallFunc_Divide_DoubleDouble_ReturnValue = KismetMathLibrary.Divide_DoubleDouble(1.0, CallFunc_GetWorldDeltaSeconds_ReturnValue)
            CallFunc_Divide_DoubleDouble_ReturnValue_1 = KismetMathLibrary.Divide_DoubleDouble(CallFunc_Divide_DoubleDouble_ReturnValue, InterpSpeed)
            CallFunc_NormalizedDeltaRotator_ReturnValue = KismetMathLibrary.NormalizedDeltaRotator(self.CamRotationCurrent, CamRotPrev)
            KismetMathLibrary.BreakRotator(CallFunc_NormalizedDeltaRotator_ReturnValue, CallFunc_BreakRotator_Roll, CallFunc_BreakRotator_Pitch, CallFunc_BreakRotator_Yaw)
            CallFunc_FClamp_Value_ImplicitCast_1 = CST_FloatToDouble(CallFunc_BreakRotator_Yaw)
            CallFunc_FClamp_ReturnValue = KismetMathLibrary.FClamp(CallFunc_FClamp_Value_ImplicitCast_1, -50.0, 50.0)
            CallFunc_Multiply_DoubleDouble_A_ImplicitCast = CST_FloatToDouble(CallFunc_BreakRotator_Pitch)
            CallFunc_Multiply_DoubleDouble_ReturnValue = KismetMathLibrary.Multiply_DoubleDouble(CallFunc_Multiply_DoubleDouble_A_ImplicitCast, -1.0)
            CallFunc_FClamp_Value_ImplicitCast = CST_FloatToDouble(CallFunc_BreakRotator_Pitch)
            CallFunc_FClamp_ReturnValue_1 = KismetMathLibrary.FClamp(CallFunc_FClamp_Value_ImplicitCast, -100.0, 100.0)
            CallFunc_FClamp_ReturnValue_2 = KismetMathLibrary.FClamp(CallFunc_Multiply_DoubleDouble_ReturnValue, -10.0, 10.0)
            CallFunc_MakeRotator_Roll_ImplicitCast = CST_DoubleToFloat(CallFunc_FClamp_ReturnValue_2)
            CallFunc_MakeRotator_Pitch_ImplicitCast = CST_DoubleToFloat(CallFunc_FClamp_ReturnValue_1)
            CallFunc_MakeRotator_Yaw_ImplicitCast = CST_DoubleToFloat(CallFunc_FClamp_ReturnValue)
            CallFunc_MakeRotator_ReturnValue = KismetMathLibrary.MakeRotator(CallFunc_MakeRotator_Roll_ImplicitCast, CallFunc_MakeRotator_Pitch_ImplicitCast, CallFunc_MakeRotator_Yaw_ImplicitCast)
            CallFunc_RInterpTo_DeltaTime_ImplicitCast = CST_DoubleToFloat(CallFunc_GetWorldDeltaSeconds_ReturnValue)
            CallFunc_RInterpTo_InterpSpeed_ImplicitCast = CST_DoubleToFloat(CallFunc_Divide_DoubleDouble_ReturnValue_1)
            CallFunc_RInterpTo_ReturnValue = KismetMathLibrary.RInterpTo(self.CamRotationRate, CallFunc_MakeRotator_ReturnValue, CallFunc_RInterpTo_DeltaTime_ImplicitCast, CallFunc_RInterpTo_InterpSpeed_ImplicitCast)
            self.CamRotationRate = CallFunc_RInterpTo_ReturnValue
            return

function UserConstructionScript  [FUNC_Event | FUNC_Public | FUNC_HasDefaults | FUNC_BlueprintCallable | FUNC_BlueprintEvent]
            CallFunc_K2_AttachToComponent_ReturnValue = self.Hazmat_Head.SceneComponent.K2_AttachToComponent(self.Hazmat_Idle, "Head", 0, 0, 1, true)
            KismetMathLibrary.BreakVector(self.Hazmat_Head.self.?, CallFunc_BreakVector_X, CallFunc_BreakVector_Y, CallFunc_BreakVector_Z)
            CallFunc_Add_DoubleDouble_ReturnValue = KismetMathLibrary.Add_DoubleDouble(CallFunc_BreakVector_Z, 2.5)
            CallFunc_MakeVector_ReturnValue = KismetMathLibrary.MakeVector(CallFunc_BreakVector_X, CallFunc_BreakVector_Y, CallFunc_Add_DoubleDouble_ReturnValue)
            self.Hazmat_Head.SceneComponent.K2_SetRelativeLocation(CallFunc_MakeVector_ReturnValue, false, CallFunc_K2_SetRelativeLocation_SweepHitResult, false)
            return
